Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the interpolation framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 423. The resulting operator achieves bound O(993/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by interpolation generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 423, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 993. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the interpolation structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 509. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 781 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the interpolation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^509 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 781-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
